# Current comparison profiles — properties, rows 150–159

[Population and scope](../property-profiles.md) · [Relationship guide](../README.md) · [Scientific account](../../BWP-S.md)

Native row order is retained. A negative or unresolved record remains a substantive judgment; a directed record creates no unlisted reverse. Shared passages link to their complete exact values.

<a id="EPM-EPM-001"></a>
## EPM:EPM-001

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-001">EPM:EPM-001</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-001</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Meaningful event identity</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM1</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S001</p></li><li><p>EPM-S014</p></li><li><p>EPM-S039</p></li><li><p>EPM-S045</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/96.md#r-96bb0281ab8e3dfc4a06a54a402129e8dec480db0337843fa2d4a5ce71056c03">Complete shared evidence limit · 96bb0281ab8e</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Event records, source/extraction/lifecycle/order semantics and collection/format policies</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A sampled event can be traced to an intelligible recorded occurrence; ambiguous classes are excluded, bounded or clearly labelled in results.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e2.md#r-e2537edb9c3efc08cc44d3ad7d45e89e46c93ec0d3831eba8274eb3a7fb28e36">Complete shared mechanism · e2537edb9c3e</a></p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Any claim that interprets event labels or counts as activities, transitions or work.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Reuse a tested event dictionary when the recording mechanism has not changed. For a question about recorded transactions only, explicitly narrow the claim instead of reconstructing an entire process.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Access to event definitions or source examples; someone able to explain recording practice; distinction between occurrence time and recording time.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/78.md#r-78e243d4c3de67179c4d51baa5c68f60ed3c6e4ee36cd36a04247c29caf2bb40">Complete shared assumptions · 78e243d4c3de</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst and domain participant decide whether an event class supports the proposed question; data custodians explain recording but do not determine organisational obligations. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/a1.md#r-a12974dc74339eb84749b518d6999a4dfb16351fc0209fb5ef9e4fdca052d9ad">Complete shared communication interaction · a12974dc7433</a></p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst and domain participant decide whether an event class supports the proposed question; data custodians explain recording but do not determine organisational obligations.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A sampled event can be traced to an intelligible recorded occurrence; ambiguous classes are excluded, bounded or clearly labelled in results.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A status row, a retrospective form entry and an activity execution can share a label while denoting different occurrences.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>An event dictionary can merely codify a mistaken assumption. The hospital study and analyst interviews show why validation must reach recording practice, not just a column name.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/4c.md#r-4c61c2df11f6521c370e7ba1ebbd40b40048ec25c431527af30532c183bf2be7">Complete shared criticism · 4c61c2df11f6</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-011.md#EPM-EPM-S001">EPM:EPM-S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S014">EPM:EPM-S014</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S016">EPM:EPM-S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S039">EPM:EPM-S039</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S045">EPM:EPM-S045</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R001</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R003</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R006</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R007</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R013</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R018</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R033</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R041</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R045</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R065</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R068</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R070</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Reuse a tested event dictionary when the recording mechanism has not changed. <dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-002">EPM:EPM-002</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-002</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Source provenance and trace-back</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM1</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S001</p></li><li><p>EPM-S014</p></li><li><p>EPM-S036</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/c8.md#r-c8ff34db36b528ac2b59121e50441065e6a613d05c6c6cf22485d3a0b6311118">Complete shared evidence limit · c8ff34db36b5</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Event records, source/extraction/lifecycle/order semantics and collection/format policies</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A reviewer can select a material finding and recover its contributing records and transformations, or see the exact provenance break.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Retain a feasible trace-back from a consequential output through selected events, source identifiers and extraction versions. Use protected pseudonymous keys where direct identifiers are unnecessary. Record which lineage edges cannot be reconstructed.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A result may be challenged, repeated, corrected or used for consequential action.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A query identifier, immutable extract and short mapping may suffice for a one-off local analysis; a full enterprise provenance platform is not required.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Stable or versioned source references; access controls; retention permission; a way to preserve transformations without disclosing private records.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/67.md#r-67412743178e47536238130ca5fc106f865b0932a317cc4cf32ef573cfb8d4db">Complete shared assumptions · 67412743178e</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst, data custodian and reviewer exchange the minimal trace evidence required to diagnose a result. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange event definitions, source examples, extraction rules and unresolved recording ambiguities between the analyst and source/domain custodians. Specific receiving decision: Analyst, data custodian and reviewer exchange the minimal trace evidence required to diagnose a result.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst, data custodian and reviewer exchange the minimal trace evidence required to diagnose a result.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A reviewer can select a material finding and recover its contributing records and transformations, or see the exact provenance break.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A reproducible diagram cannot be investigated when its events have lost source identities and transformation history.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Byte hashes establish identity, not whether the bytes mean what the analyst says. Permanent record retention can itself create privacy costs.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/bd.md#r-bd25b3f6914f75ec68eed8df2a40edd00ed7128ea22cff79cd2c23500dca8f30">Complete shared criticism · bd25b3f6914f</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-011.md#EPM-EPM-S001">EPM:EPM-S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S014">EPM:EPM-S014</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S016">EPM:EPM-S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S036">EPM:EPM-S036</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S045">EPM:EPM-S045</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R001</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R004</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R065</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A query identifier, immutable extract and short mapping may suffice for a one-off local analysis; a full enterprise provenance platform is not required.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. <a id="EPM-EPM-004"></a>
## EPM:EPM-004

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-004">EPM:EPM-004</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-004</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Activity abstraction with retained mappings</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM1</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S001</p></li><li><p>EPM-S013</p></li><li><p>EPM-S014</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/68.md#r-68473763b291bcff32c9a95db229199ebef167f1c50c6aa2c53ca2035d8dadbe">Complete shared evidence limit · 68473763b291</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Activity abstraction and analytical representation suited to a consumer</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A material finding states its abstraction level and has a traceable mapping and sensitivity result.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Choose an activity vocabulary at the level of the decision, retain the mapping from raw observations to abstract events, and test whether material conclusions survive a plausible finer or coarser alternative. Treat an abstract event as a derived object, not a newly observed fact.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Raw logs are too fine, labels combine distinct tasks, or analysis must cross systems with different granularity.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Use the native activity labels when they already answer the question. A small manually checked mapping can dominate an automated abstraction model.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A declared abstraction function or rule set; ability to inspect affected records; awareness of many-to-one and one-to-many mappings.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/26.md#r-26f27b37a26f6214fbd27611af9ed4699ed25ba1933d1a7b9473ebb4b5583841">Complete shared assumptions · 26f27b37a26f</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst proposes abstraction; domain participants test whether the resulting distinctions are sufficient for the decision. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange event definitions, source examples, extraction rules and unresolved recording ambiguities between the analyst and source/domain custodians. Specific receiving decision: Analyst proposes abstraction; domain participants test whether the resulting distinctions are sufficient for the decision.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst proposes abstraction; domain participants test whether the resulting distinctions are sufficient for the decision.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A material finding states its abstraction level and has a traceable mapping and sensitivity result.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Very fine records create uninterpretable detail; coarse labels erase distinct states, exceptions and causal alternatives.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Agreement on a readable map can conceal the disappearance of rare but important activities. Automated abstraction adds another learned hypothesis.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/bd.md#r-bde0e2a883385b93e8f3c42ab26ebb000e97f36b708ec8078f99eb25e8bab721">Complete shared criticism · bde0e2a88338</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-011.md#EPM-EPM-S001">EPM:EPM-S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S013">EPM:EPM-S013</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S014">EPM:EPM-S014</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S016">EPM:EPM-S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S039">EPM:EPM-S039</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S045">EPM:EPM-S045</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R002</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R011</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R013</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R017</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R018</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Use the native activity labels when they already answer the question. A small manually checked mapping can dominate an automated abstraction model.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>There is no general optimal granularity independent of the question and consumer.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/da.md#r-daba32aef50ea1e85004b613ea7db84e587b5361d49bf8bad1a06cd0d5bd9444">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · daba32aef50e</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retain abstraction as purposeful information loss, coupled to recoverability and explicit loss accounting rather than obligatory maximal detail. Agreement on a readable map can conceal the disappearance of rare but important activities. Automated abstraction adds another learned hypothesis.</p></dd><dt>Original namespace key</dt><dd><p>null</p></dd><dt>Original <dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-005">EPM:EPM-005</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-005</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Lifecycle-aware event interpretation</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM1</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S001</p></li><li><p>EPM-S039</p></li><li><p>EPM-S048</p></li><li><p>EPM-S050</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/29.md#r-295e99da5704708d628143424ab95ff531f557ee4c9a1207734034bec0501d15">Complete shared evidence limit · 295e99da5704</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Event records, source/extraction/lifecycle/order semantics and collection/format policies</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>Each reported interval identifies the events and lifecycle interpretation that establish its endpoints.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Identify lifecycle phases and pairing rules; separate enabled, started, suspended, resumed and completed states where actually observed. Compute service or waiting intervals only from phases whose meaning supports them, and label unobserved intervals as unknown.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Timing, utilisation, waiting, overlapping work or activity counts depend on lifecycle interpretation.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Report elapsed time between recorded milestones when service-time evidence is absent; do not impute a work interval merely to fill a dashboard.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Compatible activity-instance identifiers or justified pairing; clock interpretation; treatment of cancellation and unfinished instances.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/dd.md#r-ddd50e1e35a0981e0404af26a307d7c6e863d446c97ba81a0682df0d6c46541b">Complete shared assumptions · ddd50e1e35a0</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Performance analyst and operational staff distinguish recorded elapsed time from effort before staffing or redesign decisions. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange event definitions, source examples, extraction rules and unresolved recording ambiguities between the analyst and source/domain custodians. Specific receiving decision: Performance analyst and operational staff distinguish recorded elapsed time from effort before staffing or redesign decisions.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Performance analyst and operational staff distinguish recorded elapsed time from effort before staffing or redesign decisions.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Each reported interval identifies the events and lifecycle interpretation that establish its endpoints.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A completion timestamp alone is mistaken for work duration, or start/complete records are counted as separate full tasks.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Paired timestamps can still include inactive periods or batch entry. A sophisticated performance model does not repair misrecorded lifecycle semantics.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/36.md#r-366d59aa1b54a51bdad03dcb5e391fa115639f8e9670377f2fbe162244e14bd8">Complete shared criticism · 366d59aa1b54</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-011.md#EPM-EPM-S001">EPM:EPM-S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S039">EPM:EPM-S039</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S045">EPM:EPM-S045</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S048">EPM:EPM-S048</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S050">EPM:EPM-S050</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R002</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R033</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R045</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Report elapsed time between recorded milestones when service-time evidence is absent; do not impute a work interval merely to fill a dashboard.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>Actual labour effort may remain unavailable even with accurate start and completion times.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/fd.md#r-fd787f20ca82aeabf5d28152385d39c4bb2a1fb4abb001e2f98b055b7d6336a3">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · fd787f20ca82</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Narrow duration claims to observable intervals and use richer lifecycle analysis only when the data and decision justify it. Paired timestamps can still include inactive periods or batch entry. A sophisticated performance model does not repair misrecorded lifecycle semantics.</p></dd><dt>Original namespace key</dt><dd><p>null</p></dd><dt>Original <a id="EPM-EPM-006"></a>
## EPM:EPM-006

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-006">EPM:EPM-006</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-006</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Ordering proportional to temporal evidence</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM1</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S025</p></li><li><p>EPM-S039</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e6.md#r-e60bc3c8bb88ae12b310c5f5864b89d3f6f6876e0983508af7d834c81ccbaf50">Complete shared evidence limit · e60bc3c8bb88</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Event records, source/extraction/lifecycle/order semantics and collection/format policies</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A conclusion is either invariant across plausible orders or qualified by the orders on which it depends.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Use the strongest ordering supported by the evidence: a total order where justified, an explicit partial order or bounded alternatives otherwise. Keep tie-breaking separate from observed chronology and test sensitivity when an algorithm requires a sequence.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Timestamp ties, mixed granularity, asynchronous systems, uncertain timestamps or consequential order-sensitive findings.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Ignore order for an order-insensitive question, or flag the few ambiguous pairs instead of enumerating every possible trace.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Knowledge of clocks, recording delay and lifecycle; an algorithm able to express uncertainty or a declared approximation.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/7f.md#r-7f228ab3a8bea09d59294affed5ca5d23a7b98cc6c9d213c5189c94d61147259">Complete shared assumptions · 7f228ab3a8be</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst exposes ordering alternatives to the consumer; system timestamps alone do not settle the real chronology. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange event definitions, source examples, extraction rules and unresolved recording ambiguities between the analyst and source/domain custodians. Specific receiving decision: Analyst exposes ordering alternatives to the consumer; system timestamps alone do not settle the real chronology.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst exposes ordering alternatives to the consumer; system timestamps alone do not settle the real chronology.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A conclusion is either invariant across plausible orders or qualified by the orders on which it depends.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Coarse clocks and recording delays induce an arbitrary total order, creating false directly-follows, concurrency or conformance claims.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Enumerating realisations can be expensive; asserted uncertainty sets can omit the true order. <a id="EPM-EPM-007"></a>
## EPM:EPM-007

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-007">EPM:EPM-007</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-007</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Quality repair without erasing uncertainty</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM1</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S039</p></li><li><p>EPM-S045</p></li><li><p>EPM-S025</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/fa.md#r-fa8a0b1e4c8f1698d1854adc2ad7d208447268bba2081aa0064532e904af434e">Complete shared evidence limit · fa8a0b1e4c8f</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Event records, source/extraction/lifecycle/order semantics and collection/format policies</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>Material repairs have reasons and consequences; users can identify which conclusions require the repair to be correct.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Classify suspected defects, keep the original or a recoverable trace, justify each material repair, and compare important results with unrepaired or alternative treatments. Where correction is unsupported, carry uncertainty or decline the affected inference.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Detected defects materially threaten the stated question, not merely because a quality metric is nonzero.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Annotate an isolated uncertain record, narrow a claim, or tolerate a known harmless defect rather than rebuild the whole log.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A defect hypothesis linked to recording behaviour; safeguards against deleting meaningful cases; separate raw and derived states.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/dc.md#r-dc5dd5c103e8c804513a805590fb72d3df08ae499a7f4221922952e48fcd1425">Complete shared assumptions · dc5dd5c103e8</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst and data custodian review repair rules; domain participants distinguish atypical work from recording error. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange event definitions, source examples, extraction rules and unresolved recording ambiguities between the analyst and source/domain custodians. Specific receiving decision: Analyst and data custodian review repair rules; domain participants distinguish atypical work from recording error.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst and data custodian review repair rules; domain participants distinguish atypical work from recording error.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Material repairs have reasons and consequences; users can identify which conclusions require the repair to be correct.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Missing values, duplicates and errors distort models, but aggressive cleaning erases genuine exceptions and the evidence of uncertainty.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Data-cleaning success measured only by higher conformance is circular. The ED case documents recording imperfections without proving that every atypical trace should be corrected.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/d5.md#r-d5bdfe470901cb02e9b04f52e2cd905cba7f1ccdda7a385fee0ac88d5eab3e71">Complete shared criticism · d5bdfe470901</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S016">EPM:EPM-S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S025">EPM:EPM-S025</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S039">EPM:EPM-S039</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S045">EPM:EPM-S045</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S059">EPM:EPM-S059</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R003</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R065</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Annotate an isolated uncertain record, narrow a claim, or tolerate a known harmless defect rather than rebuild the whole log.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>The true value of a missing event is often not identifiable from the surrounding trace.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e8.md#r-e86489f73b506c413649ca74821b892ae51ec961cbf5f64a32f46ea79f97d2e3">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · e86489f73b50</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Replace universal cleansing with loss-aware, hypothesis-tested repair and a valid option to leave an event uncertain. Data-cleaning success measured only by higher conformance is circular. The ED case documents recording imperfections without proving that every atypical trace should be corrected.</p></dd><dt>Original namespace key</dt><dd><p>null</p></dd><dt>Original
